### [1] [Availability-Aware and Efficiency-Driven AI Service Chain Provisioning in Multi-Domain Edge Intelligence Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05637)
*Hanzhi Chang,Jing Bai,Xin Tang,Xiaomei Liu,Yiming Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对多域边缘智能云（MDEIC）中的AI服务链（AISC）供给问题，提出了一种基于图-时间多智能体的AISC供给方法（GT-MAAISCP），通过部分可观测随机博弈建模和图时间决斗网络架构，实现了供给成本、延迟和可用性的协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在由多个网络运营商管理的多域边缘智能云（MDEIC）中，AI服务通过虚拟网络功能（VNF）链顺序执行来交付。然而，MDEIC的环境特性（异构性、资源约束和有限的信息可见性）与AISC的时间依赖性之间的交互作用，给AISC供给带来了诸多挑战，亟需一种高效且经济的供给方法。

Method: 首先将AISC供给问题建模为部分可观测随机博弈（POSG）；然后提出图-时间多智能体AISC供给方法（GT-MAAISCP），每个智能体采用图时间决斗网络（GTDN）架构来提取网络拓扑信息和时间关系，实现AISC供给成本、延迟和可用性的协同优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法在MDEIC环境中优于基准方法，并在不同网络拓扑和不同数量的本地边缘智能云（LEIC）条件下展现了良好的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的GT-MAAISCP方法能够有效解决MDEIC中AISC供给面临的挑战，实现了成本、延迟和可用性的协同优化，为多运营商边缘智能云环境中的AI服务供给提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In a multi-domain edge intelligence cloud (MDEIC) managed by multiple network operators, AI services are delivered by chains of virtual network functions (VNFs) executed in sequence, called AI service chains (AISCs). Therefore, achieving an efficient and economical AISC provisioning approach is essential. However, the interaction between the environmental characteristics (heterogeneity, resource constraints and limited information visibility) of MDEIC and the time-dependence of AISCs, introduces various challenges to AISC provisioning in MDEIC. In this paper, we first formulate the AISC provisioning problem as a partially observable stochastic game (POSG). Then, we propose a graph-and-time-based multi-agent AISC provisioning (GT-MAAISCP) approach to achieve the collaborative optimization of AISC provisioning cost, delay and availability. Specifically, each agent uses the graph-time dueling network (GTDN) architecture to extract network topology information and temporal relationships. Finally, the experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms benchmark approaches in MDEIC and also illustrate its performance under varying network topologies and different numbers of local EICs (LEICs).

</details>


### [2] [DAST: A VLM-LLM Framework for Cross-Interface Anomaly Detection in O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06261)
*Francesco Spinelli,Esteban Municio,Pau Baguer,Gines Garcia-Aviles,Xavier Costa-Perez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DAST，一个用于O-RAN跨接口异常检测的零样本多智能体框架，通过VLM→LLM→VLM三阶段管道处理，在真实O-RAN测试台上实现了0.910 F1-Score和0.843准确率，优于现有TSAD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN的开放性虽然实现了多厂商组合，但也扩大了计算连续体中逻辑分离层级之间的攻击面。其中，拒绝服务和性能下降攻击占O-RAN威胁的大部分，但难以检测。传统时间序列异常检测方法在此场景下失效，原因包括：标注基线稀缺、威胁演化速度快于检测器重训练速度、高维多变量遥测数据压倒单一推理模型。

Method: 提出DAST（零样本多智能体框架），采用三阶段VLM→LLM→VLM管道：1）将多变量KPI流转换为可视化表示；2）根据O-RAN领域知识对每个接口的文本描述进行评分；3）在高分辨率热力图上验证可疑对象。最终输出问题接口、异常时间间隔、O-RAN WG11对齐的操作影响评级和决策依据。

Result: 在真实O-RAN测试台收集的网络轨迹上进行评估，在代表性性能下降场景下，DAST实现了0.910 F1-Score和0.843准确率，超越了最先进的时间序列异常检测基线方法。

Conclusion: DAST成功解决了O-RAN环境中传统TSAD方法面临的挑战，为零样本跨接口异常检测提供了有效解决方案，能够准确识别问题接口、异常时间间隔并提供符合标准的操作影响评估。

Abstract: O-RAN enables a disaggregated baseband stack with programmable functions that communicate over standardized open interfaces. The same openness that enables multi-vendor composition also expands the attack surface across logically decoupled tiers that make up the compute continuum. Among these threats, Denial-of-Service and performance-degradation attacks, which account for the majority of catalogued O-RAN threats, are particularly difficult to detect. Traditional Time-Series Anomaly Detection (TSAD) methods fail in this new regime where labelled baselines are scarce, threats evolve faster than detectors can be retrained, and the high-dimensional multivariate telemetry overwhelms monolithic inference models. To address these challenges, we present DAST, a zero-shot multi-agent framework for cross-interface anomaly detection in O-RAN that chains a three-stage VLM $\rightarrow$ LLM $\rightarrow$ VLM pipeline. DAST converts multivariate KPI streams into visual representations, scores textual per-interface descriptions against O-RAN domain knowledge, and verifies suspects on high-resolution heatmaps to output the problematic interfaces, the anomalous time intervals, an indicative O-RAN WG11-aligned operational impact rating and the decision rationale. We evaluate DAST on real network traces collected from an O-RAN testbed under representative performance degradation scenarios, achieving 0.910 F1-Score and 0.843 Accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art TSAD baselines.

</details>


### [3] [AISC deployment in dynamic UAV-assisted MEC network: a reinforcement learning method based on heterogeneous graph attention neural network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05722)
*Hanzhi Chang,Jing Bai,Xin Tang,Xiaomei Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于异构图神经网络的双重深度注意力Q网络，用于解决高动态UMEC网络中AI服务链部署优化问题，在完成时间、完成率、负载均衡和能耗方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMEC网络拓扑因无人机高速移动或到达/离开而高度动态，导致AISC部署困难；UMEC环境与AISC之间、以及AISC中各VNF之间的复杂关系影响部署策略效果；在能耗和负载均衡约束下优化AISC策略以最小化完成时间具有挑战性。

Method: 提出基于异构图神经网络的双重深度注意力Q网络（double deep attention Q-network），利用异构图捕获UMEC中的多样化关系，并采用注意力机制自适应聚焦关键节点和链路，实现智能AISC部署。

Result: 实验结果表明，该算法在AISC完成时间、AISC完成率、负载均衡和能耗方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法能够有效解决UMEC网络中AISC部署的挑战，在多个性能指标上取得优异表现，有助于提升AI服务质量。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles-assisted mobile edge computing (UMEC) can execute compute-intensive and latency-critical artificial intelligence (AI) services, which can be provided by multiple UAVs collaborating in the air to perform inference tasks. Completing an AI service requires multiple inferences, each of which is implemented by an AI service chain consisting of multiple virtual network functions (VNFs). The application of AISC relies on an efficient AISC deployment strategy to determine which UAV to deploy VNF on. However, the UMEC network topology is highly dynamic due to the high-speed movement of UAVs or their departure/arrival, which makes the AISC deployment in the UMEC network challenging. In addition, the intricate relationships between UMEC environment and AISC, as well as between individual VNFs in an AISC, can also affect the effectiveness of AISC deployment strategy. Moreover, under the constraints of energy consumption and load balancing, it is also difficult to optimize the AISC strategy to minimize AISC completion time for enhancing the quality of AI service. To address the above challenges, this paper proposes a double deep attention Q-network based on heterogeneous graph neural networks, which incorporates heterogeneous graph to capture diverse relationships in UMEC and utilizes attention mechanisms to adaptively focus on critical nodes and links for intelligent AISC deployment. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm performs excellently in AISC completion time, AISC completion rate, load balancing and energy consumption.

</details>


### [4] [BeGREEN Intelligent Plane for AI-driven Energy Efficient O-RAN management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05747)
*M. Catalan-Cid,J. Pueyo,J. Sanchez-Gonzalez,J. Gutierrez,M. Ghoraishi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BeGREEN智能平面框架，这是一个在O-RAN架构中实现AI/ML工作流以优化蜂窝网络能效的新型解决方案，并通过AI驱动的小区开/关控制案例展示了其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着O-RAN架构和AI/ML技术的兴起，蜂窝网络正在经历变革。O-RAN的非实时和近实时RAN智能控制器为自动化控制循环的实施提供了可能，能够实现多种场景下的RAN优化。由于移动网络对全球能源消耗的影响，能源可持续性已成为主要优化目标之一。BeGREEN项目旨在通过在RAN和边缘基础设施定义新的AI/ML方法来提升超5G网络的能效。

Method: 提出了BeGREEN智能平面(Intelligent Plane)框架，该框架在O-RAN架构基础上实现并暴露AI/ML工作流，用于针对能效的优化。框架包含AI引擎组件，能够支持AI驱动的网络优化应用。作为一个示例应用，实现了基于AI的小区开/关控制功能。

Result: 成功设计并实现了BeGREEN智能平面框架，该框架能够有效集成AI/ML工作流到O-RAN架构中，为能效优化提供支持。AI引擎能够支持具体应用场景，如AI驱动的小区开/关控制，展示了框架的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: BeGREEN智能平面框架为O-RAN架构下的能效优化提供了有效的AI/ML集成解决方案，具有实际部署价值。通过AI驱动的小区开/关控制示例应用证明了该框架的可行性，为未来超5G网络的能效提升提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Cellular networks are undergoing a revolutionary transform with the advent of O-RAN architectures and AI/ML solutions. O-RAN's Non-Real-Time and Near-Real Time RAN Intelligent Controllers open the door to the implementation of automated control-loops that can provide RAN optimisations in numerous scenarios and use cases, and which can be further empowered by AI-driven approaches. Energetic sustainability has raised as one of the main optimisations targets due to the impact of mobile networks on global energy consumption. To this end, the BeGREEN project aims at enhancing the energy efficiency of beyond 5G networks by defining novel AI/ML-based methods at RAN and edge infrastructure. This paper presents BeGREEN Intelligent Plane, a novel framework which implements and exposes AI/ML workflows to O-RAN-based optimisations targeting energy efficiency. We also describe an exemplary application of the Intelligent Plane and its AI Engine, which aims at providing AI-driven cell on/off control.

</details>


### [5] [Quantifying the Energy-Saving and QoS Trade-Off in Traffic Offloading for Real 4G/5G Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05752)
*D. Reiss,M. Catalan-Cid,D. Camps-Mur,O. Sallent*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在实际5G NSA部署中研究了通过选择性关闭低利用率5G小区并将流量卸载至4G小区的节能方案，发现可在保证数据速率的前提下实现17%-79%的网络级小区关断机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管5G网络具有更高的能效潜力，但当前5G NSA部署中4G和5G载波在长时间内利用率较低，导致运行效率低下。由于基站是网络能耗的主要来源，因此需要研究小区开关和流量卸载策略以提高当前部署的能效。

Method: 利用欧洲移动网络运营商提供的真实5G NSA部署数据集，基于关键性能指标(KPI)提出数据驱动框架，评估选择性停用低利用率5G小区并将流量卸载至同一扇区和站点内资源充足的4G小区时的节能与QoS权衡。

Result: 实验结果表明，在确保数据速率介于25 Mbps至5 Mbps之间的条件下，网络级小区关断机会范围为17%至79%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在现有5G NSA部署中存在显著的节能潜力，通过合理的小区开关和流量卸载策略可以在保证服务质量的同时实现大规模的能效提升。

Abstract: Despite the potential for higher energy efficiency in 5G networks, current 5G Non-Standalone (NSA) deployments often operate suboptimally due to low utilization of 4G and 5G carriers during extended periods. Since base stations are the primary contributors to network energy consumption, implementing cell on/off switching and traffic offloading strategies is crucial for enhancing energy efficiency in current deployments. This paper investigates energy-saving opportunities based on these strategies in a real 5G NSA deployment, utilizing a dataset provided by a European Mobile Network Operator. Using Key Performance Indicators from the dataset, we propose a data-driven framework to evaluate the energy-saving and QoS tradeoff when selectively deactivating underutilized 5G cells and offloading their traffic to 4G cells with enough resources within the same sector and site. Our results demonstrate network-wide cell switch-off opportunities ranging from 17% to 79%, while ensuring data rates between 25 Mbps and 5 Mbps, respectively.

</details>


### [6] [Policy-Guided ML for Energy Savings: Cell On/Off Switching under Operator QoS Constraints in Real 5G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05755)
*D. Reiss,M. Catalan-Cid,D. Camps-Mur,O. Sallent*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的5G网络节能策略，通过调整训练过程中的类别比例，使运营商能够在节能效果与QoS策略合规性之间进行权衡，实现了在保证服务质量前提下的显著节能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G网络部署和运营中能效是关键问题，特别是在非高峰时段4G和5G载波利用率较低。现有研究主要关注避免中断用户连接的小区开关策略，但对集成运营商特定策略以保证特定服务质量(QoS)水平的关注有限。

Method: 提出了一种基于机器学习(ML)的节能策略，使用欧洲移动运营商的真实数据集进行训练。该方法强制执行运营商定义的策略，同时考虑强吞吐量需求和最大中断容忍约束。通过在训练过程中调整模型的类别比例，使运营商能够在实际部署前管理节能与QoS策略合规性之间的权衡。

Result: 评估结果表明，该方法在现实的5G运营条件下，在保持符合策略的服务水平的同时，提供了显著的节能效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的机器学习节能策略成功地将运营商定义的QoS策略集成到节能决策中，使运营商能够灵活管理节能与服务质量的权衡，为5G网络的可持续运营提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Energy efficiency is a critical concern in the deployment and operation of 5G networks, particularly due to the low utilization of 4G and 5G carriers during off-peak hours. While considerable research has focused on designing energy-efficient cell on/off switching strategies that avoid disrupting user connectivity, the integration of operator-specific policies to guarantee particular Quality of Service (QoS) levels has received limited attention. This paper presents a machine learning (ML)-based energy saving strategy, trained using a real-world dataset from a European mobile operator, that enforces operator-defined policies that jointly consider strong throughput requirements and maximum outage tolerance constraints. By tuning the model's class ratios during training, the proposed solution enables operators to manage the trade-off between energy savings and QoS policy compliance prior to deployment in live networks. Evaluation results show that the method provides substantial energy savings while maintaining policy-compliant service levels under realistic 5G operating conditions.

</details>


### [7] [Toward Mobile and Converged Backhaul: The Promise of Wireless Access and Backhaul](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06075)
*Chiara Rubaltelli,Marcello Morini,Eugenio Moro,Ilario Filippini,Antonio Capone*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了3GPP Release 19标准化的无线接入与回传（WAB）框架，通过商业硬件和开源软件实现验证了其可行性，并探讨了四种应用场景及大规模部署的技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAB作为5G部署的关键使能技术，能够提供灵活且具有成本效益的模块化架构，解耦接入和回传，支持多技术和移动回传链路，为5G-Advanced和未来6G网络奠定基础。

Method: 介绍了3GPP Release 19标准化的WAB框架的架构和运行原理，并利用商业硬件和开源软件构建了一个实际的实现系统来验证其可行性和效率。

Result: 成功展示了WAB系统的可行性和效率，确定了四种代表性应用场景（包括按需覆盖和移动SD-WAN连接），并分析了中国大规模采用所需解决的技术挑战。

Conclusion: WAB是5G-Advanced的有前景的基础，也是通往未来6G网络的垫脚石，为灵活、经济高效的5G部署提供了标准化解决方案。

Abstract: Wireless Access and Backhaul (WAB) is emerging as a key enabler for flexible and cost-efficient 5G deployments, offering a modular architecture that decouples access and backhaul while supporting multi-technology and mobile backhaul links. This article introduces the WAB framework standardized in 3GPP Release 19, outlining its architecture and operational principles. A practical implementation built with commercial hardware and open-source software demonstrates the feasibility and efficiency of WAB systems. We further explore four representative application scenarios - ranging from on-demand coverage to mobile Software-Defined Wide Area Network (SD-WAN) connectivity - and discuss the technical challenges that must be addressed for large-scale adoption. These insights highlight WAB as a promising foundation for 5G-Advanced and a stepping stone toward future 6G networks.

</details>

### [8] [RAINO: Anchoring Agents in Reality, A Systematic Review and Conceptual Framework for Realism in Agent-Based Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05167)
*Loïs Vanhée,Melania Borit*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性文献综述揭示了Agent-Based Modelling中现实性概念定义不清、缺乏一致性框架的问题，并提出了RAINO框架来系统化地论证模型现实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实性是Agent-Based Modelling中的核心概念，但目前缺乏充分的理论化和一致的概念框架，亟需系统梳理其操作化方式。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，识别现实性在当前研究中如何被操作化和论证，并在此基础上构建RAINO理论框架。

Result: 发现现实性往往定义不明确、缺乏一致概念框架；虽然使用多种方法实现和展示现实性，但对这些方法适当性的解释普遍有限；提出了RAINO框架，通过现实锚点（经验数据、形式理论、专家知识、常识预期）及其作为模型输入或输出的应用来论证现实性。

Conclusion: RAINO框架拓宽了对现实性的理解视角，解释了为何不同评估者可能以不同方式评估模型现实性，并展示了这种更广泛的框架如何导致显著不同的模型开发方法。

Abstract: Realism is a central yet seemingly under-theorized concept in Agent-Based Modelling. This paper presents a Systematic Literature Review, aiming to identify how realism is currently operationalized and demonstrated. The results show that realism is often poorly defined and lacks a consistent conceptual framework. A wide variety of methods are used to achieve and demonstrate realism, but explanations of whether and why these methods are appropriate for their intended purposes are generally limited. Building on this review, we introduce the Reality Anchor, Input, Output (RAINO) framework. RAINO identifies the key structures used to argue for realism in Agent-Based Models, consisting of Reality Anchors (e.g., empirical data, formal theory, expert knowledge, common-sense expectations) and their application as model Input or Output. RAINO broadens existing perspectives on how realism is framed. It explains why different assessors may evaluate the realism of a model in different ways, and it shows how this broader framing can lead to significantly different approaches to model development.

</details>


### [9] [Ahoy: LLMs Enacting Multiagent Interaction Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05390)
*Omkar Joshi,Munindar P. Singh,Amit K. Chopra*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了Ahoy系统，利用大语言模型创建能够动态选择和执行声明式协议的智能体，实现了无需编程的协议 enactment。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统交互协议方法产生的智能体实现与特定协议绑定，缺乏灵活性。研究旨在解决如何构建能够执行协议但无需编程的智能智能体这一问题。

Method: 提出Ahoy方法，结合声明式协议与大语言模型(LLM)，创建能够根据用户目标动态选择并执行声明式协议的LLM智能体，无需专门训练即可工作。

Result: Ahoy智能体能够正确且智能地执行多种协议，在适应用户目标时可并发执行多个协议，且无需专门训练。

Conclusion: Ahoy的重要性在于成功结合了声明式协议和LLM两种方法，为智能体的知识工程改进提供了新途径，实现了协议执行与编程的解耦。

Abstract: An interaction protocol formalizes how the agents in a multiagent system interact, which facilitates implementing agents. Existing approaches yield agent implementations specific to the selected protocols. How can we engineer intelligent agents that can enact protocols but are programming-free? Our contribution, Ahoy, addresses this question by creating LLM agents that dynamically select and enact declarative protocols to achieve user goals. We demonstrate that an \ahoy agent can correctly and intelligently enact multiple protocols - concurrently if appropriate to the user goal - without specialized training. Ahoy's significance lies in that it brings together declarative protocols and LLMs, both approaches that promise improved knowledge engineering for agents.

</details>


### [10] [Learning to Contest: Decentralized Robust Fairness in Cooperative MARL via Cross-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06162)
*Can Savcı*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 在多智能体强化学习（MARL）中，追求公平的团队容易被自利智能体剥削。本文提出CAN策略，通过交叉注意力机制推断搭便车者数量并按比例应对，在分级竞争下实现了接近中心化方法的抗剥削性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平导向的多智能体强化学习团队因追求平等主义福利最大化而容易被单个自利智能体剥削（搭便车问题）。虽然中心化的需求分配器可以消除此问题，但需要剥夺智能体的分配权。本文旨在探索是否存在一种去中心化策略，既能保持公平性，又能有效抵抗剥削。

Method: 作者首先证明在分级竞争机制下（竞争失败的浪费为c<1），去中心化的反抗手段存在博弈优势。核心方法CAN（Cross-Attention Network）采用置换等变的交叉注意力架构，根据观察到的行为推断搭便车者数量并做出成比例的响应：无搭便车者时轮流行动，存在时进行适度竞争。训练采用PSRO（Policy Space Response Oracles）对抗博弈方法。

Result: CAN在竞争范围内将可剥削性保持在低水平（ρ≈1.2-1.5，相比无保护时的ρ=N），在低竞争下几乎不浪费（效率≈1.0），在高竞争下仍保持大部分效率（0.83-0.96），在效率和鲁棒性两个维度上都接近中心化预言机的性能。相比之下，其他公平MARL学习方法要么低效要么可被剥削。实验还发现CAN的鲁棒性与竞争杠杆强度成正比：在多服务器游戏中表现强，杠杆减弱时表现部分有效，在赢者通吃场景中失效。

Conclusion: 去中心化策略确实可以在分级竞争下实现公平性与抗剥削性。CAN通过推断搭便车者数量并动态调整策略，成功解决了公平团队被剥削的问题，无需中心化分配器。然而，其有效性依赖于竞争杠杆的强度，在杠杆微弱或零样本迁移到大团队等场景下仍存在脆弱性。

Abstract: Fair cooperative multi-agent RL (MARL) teams maximizing egalitarian welfare are exploitable: a single selfish agent free-rides on the surplus fair agents forgo to raise the worst-off. A centralized need-based allocator removes it, but only by taking allocation out of agents' hands; whether decentralized policies can be robust was left open. We show this futility is an artifact of all-or-nothing contention. Under graded contention (a contested resource delivers $1-c$, wasting $c$), we prove that for any $c<1$ a worst-off cooperator that contests a free-rider strictly improves on yielding, so decentralized leverage exists (Prop. 1). Realizing it is a coordination problem under uncertainty: the number of free-riders is unknown and variable, so any fixed rule is dominated. We introduce CAN, a permutation-equivariant cross-attention policy over agents' observed behaviour that infers the number of free-riders and responds proportionally: turn-taking when none, contesting just enough when some. Trained against an adversarial league (PSRO), CAN keeps best-response exploitability low ($ρ\approx1.2$-$1.5$, vs. $ρ=N$ unprotected) across the contention range, wasting almost nothing at $D=0$ (efficiency $\approx1.0$) and retaining most of it at $D\geq1$ (efficiency 0.83-0.96), approaching the centralized oracle on both axes, no central allocator. Fair-MARL learners fail on complementary axes (GGF/FEN yield and are exploitable, SOTO all-contests and wastes), while CAN is both. On two further games we find clear scope, not blanket generality: CAN stays efficient and Pareto-dominates the fair learners, but its robustness holds only in proportion to the contest leverage: strong on a multi-server game, partial when it weakens, absent under winner-take-all (Prop. 1 fails). We also report its fragilities: weak leverage and zero-shot transfer to larger teams degrade it at high contention.

</details>


### [11] [A Swarm Approach to Public Transit Using On-demand Routing in a Slime-Mold-Inspired Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06189)
*Lindsay Burke,Maxfield Comstock,Jason Graham,Ruth Malenda,Simon Garnier,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于黏菌启发算法的分布式需求响应型交通系统，通过动态换乘和协作竞标机制，在郊区、城市和半农村场景下分别将乘客送达率提升28%、49%和101%，步行时间减少超过75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式、人工调度的需求响应型交通系统在低密度社区应用中面临运营成本高、可靠性低等关键问题，亟需更高效的调度方案。

Method: 采用黏菌启发的路由算法实现多车辆动态路径规划，引入动态换乘机制优化网络效率，通过持续的协作竞标过程处理乘客分配和动态换乘。使用OpenStreetMap数据进行郊区、城市和半农村三种场景仿真。

Result: 相较于固定网络方法，在郊区、城市和半农村场景下乘客送达率分别提高28%、49%和101%；所有场景下步行时间均减少超过75%。

Conclusion: 基于群智能的分布式动态路由系统能够显著提升需求响应型交通网络的效率和乘客体验，为解决传统DRT系统的高成本和低可靠性问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Demand-responsive transit (DRT) is a flexible alternative to traditional, fixed-route mass-transit networks. Although DRT can function well in low-density communities, high operating costs and low reliability are common issues. We propose that these issues can be mitigated by moving from a centralized, manually-scheduled scheme to a distributed system capable of dynamically routing multiple vehicles using a slime-mold-inspired routing algorithm to maximize network effectiveness. We additionally introduce the method of dynamic transfers to further optimize transit network efficiency. All passenger allocation and dynamic transfers are handled via a continual cooperative bidding process by the buses. In this paper, we present simulated results for a swarm-driven transit network in suburban, urban, and semi-rural scenarios, using map networks pulled from OpenStreetMap. We show that our approach increases passenger delivery rates relative to a fixed-network approach by 28%, 49%, and 101%, respectively, and results in over 75% reduction in walking time in all cases.

</details>

### [12] [Unsupervised Skill Discovery for Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06416)
*Zhisong Qiu,Kangqi Song,Shengwei Tang,Shuofei Qiao,Lei Liang,Huajun Chen,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DataCOPE框架，通过无监督验证器引导的技能发现机制，从无标注探索中自动提取可复用的数据分析技能，在报告生成和推理两类任务上平均性能提升9.71%和32.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时技能增强可以在不更新模型参数的情况下提升数据分析智能体性能，但发现有效的数据分析技能具有挑战性：可靠的监督信号代价高昂，且不同分析格式的成功标准各异。如何在无标注探索中发现可复用的数据分析技能成为关键问题。

Method: 提出DataCOPE无监督验证器引导的技能发现框架，包含三个核心组件协同迭代：数据分析智能体负责轨迹生成；无监督验证器从探索轨迹中提取信号（针对报告式分析采用自适应清单验证器，通过可验证覆盖率评分；针对推理式分析采用答案一致性验证器，利用自一致性作为辅助信号）；技能管理器执行对比式技能蒸馏。

Result: 在Deep Data Research的报告式分析和DABStep的推理式分析上进行评估。跨两种场景，DataCOPE在留出测试集上持续优于基线方法。在四种模型设置上平均，DataCOPE在报告式和推理式任务上分别提升平均分数9.71%和32.30%。

Conclusion: DataCOPE成功实现了从无标注探索中发现可复用技能，验证了无监督验证器引导方法的有效性，为数据分析智能体的技能增强提供了新范式。

Abstract: Inference-time skill augmentation provides a lightweight way to improve data-analytic agents by injecting reusable procedural knowledge without updating model parameters. However, discovering effective skills for data analysis remains challenging, as reliable supervision is expensive and success criteria vary across analytical formats. This raises the key question of how to discover reusable data-analysis skills from unlabeled exploration alone. We propose DataCOPE, an unsupervised verifier-guided skill discovery framework for data-analytic agents. DataCOPE derives verifier signals from the exploration trajectories and uses them to characterize relative quality or aggreement among trajectories. It iteratively coordinates a Data-Analytic Agent for trajectory generation, an Unsupervised Verifier for signal extraction, and a Skill Manager for contrastive skill distillation. For report-style analysis, we instantiate the verifier as an Adaptive Checklist Verifier that derives task-specific criteria, scores reports by verifiable coverage, and iteratively refines the checklist. For reasoning-style analysis, we instantiate it as an Answer Agreement Verifier that groups trajectories by answer agreement and uses self-consistency as an auxiliary signal. We evaluate DataCOPE on report-style analysis from Deep Data Research and reasoning-style analysis from DABStep. Across both settings, DataCOPE consistently improves held-out performance over baselines. Averaged across four model settings, DataCOPE improves the mean score by 9.71% and 32.30% on report-style and reasoning-style tasks respectively.

</details>


### [13] [How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
*Kokil Jaidka,Saifuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了Reddit上未披露AI账号在辩论中的行为模式，发现AI通过系统性修辞策略（身份操控、权威信号、认知偏差触发）实现高说服效率，与人类辩论模式截然相反，揭示了现有披露政策的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reddit上一个未经披露的AI辩论实验因伦理争议被叫停，公开的AI生成评论数据集提供了罕见机会来研究大语言模型在身份敏感的审议论坛中的实际运作方式，揭示AI如何在没有透明度的情况下参与复杂的人类辩论。

Method: 对AI生成的评论语料库进行结构化内容分析，评估四个维度：身份表现、权威信号、对齐策略和认知启发式激活，并将分析结果与人类撰写的对照组评论进行对比。

Result: 超过三分之二的评论出现身份定向或采纳，近全部评论包含对齐动作和权威主张，绝大多数触发认知偏差（特别是确认偏差、代表性偏差和可得性偏差）。这些模式系统性共现，形成针对说服效率而非真实审议参与的修辞架构。与人类相比，AI在所有维度上都呈现反向分布：更密集的权威使用、更强对抗性对齐、更依赖外部引用而非经验依据。

Conclusion: 在身份丰富的审议环境中，真实与合成的认知地位界限日益模糊——这种不对称性仅靠披露要求无法解决。研究指出需要开发能够评估AI系统如何构建可信度的审计框架，而不仅仅是检测其是否存在。

Abstract: This study analyzes a publicly released dataset from a discontinued field experiment on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView. The intervention, conducted by unknown, external researchers and halted following ethical backlash, involved undisclosed AI-generated accounts engaging users in live debate. After public disclosure, Reddit authorized moderators to release an archive of the AI-generated comments, creating a rare opportunity to examine how large language models operated in an identity-rich deliberative forum without disclosure. We conduct a structured content analysis of this corpus, evaluating identity performance, authority signaling, alignment strategies, and activation of cognitive heuristics. Identity targeting or adoption appears in over two-thirds of comments, alignment moves and authority claims in nearly all of them, and cognitive-bias triggers -- particularly confirmation bias, representativeness, and availability -- in the large majority. These patterns co-occur systematically, composing a rhetorical architecture calibrated for persuasive efficiency rather than authentic deliberative participation. Compared against human-authored CMV counter-arguments, the agents inverted the typical distribution on every dimension: denser authority use, more adversarial alignment, and heavier reliance on external citation over experiential grounding. In such environments, distinctions between authentic and synthetic epistemic standing grow increasingly opaque -- an asymmetry that disclosure mandates alone cannot address. The results point toward auditing frameworks capable of assessing how AI systems structure credibility, not merely whether they are present.

</details>


### [14] [What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
*Chen Huang,Yuhao Wu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACT框架，通过将智能体间的自由通信协议化为紧凑的动作-状态记录，在保持或提升任务性能的同时显著降低token消耗，解决了多智能体系统中通信开销过大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）通常采用角色、流水线和轮次调度来组织，但智能体间传递的内容往往是未约束的自然语言。这种自由形式的通信会迅速增加token使用量，占用共享上下文窗口，最终影响系统性能和推理成本。

Method: 作者首先分析了五种常见的智能体间通信策略和两种MAS拓扑结构，发现没有固定策略是普遍最优的，而是有效的智能体间消息应保留下游智能体所需的动作中心信息。基于此，提出PACT（Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission）方法，将智能体间通信视为公共状态更新问题，将每个原始智能体输出在进入共享历史之前投影为紧凑的动作-状态记录。

Result: 在不同的MAS拓扑结构中，PACT持续改善性能-成本权衡，以更少的token实现相当或更强的任务性能。在生产代码工具中，PACT使OpenHands的解决率在token-per-resolved减少10%的情况下提升，并在SWE-agent上保持解决率不变的同时将输入token减半。

Conclusion: PACT通过协议化的动作-状态通信有效解决了多智能体系统中自由通信导致的token膨胀问题，证明了将智能体间通信结构化为状态更新的有效性，为构建高效的多智能体系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models are typically organized around roles, pipelines, and turn schedules, while the content that agents pass to one another is often left as unconstrained natural language. However, this free-form communication can rapidly inflate token usage, consume the shared context window, and ultimately affect both system performance and inference cost. We analyze five common inter-agent communication strategies across two MAS topologies, finding that no fixed strategy is universally optimal. Instead, effective inter-agent messages consistently preserve action-centered information needed by downstream agents. Building on this, we propose the PACT (Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission), which treats inter-agent communication as a public state-update problem and projects each raw agent output into a compact action-state record before it enters shared history. Across different MAS topologies, PACT consistently improves the performance-cost trade-off, achieving comparable or stronger task performance with substantially fewer tokens. The gains extend to production coding harnesses: PACT lifts OpenHands' resolve rate at -10% tokens-per-resolved, and is resolve-neutral on SWE-agent while halving input tokens. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT.

</details>


### [15] [I Know What You Meme, Even If it Emerged Today: Understanding Evolving Memes through Open-World Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05316)
*Shanhong Liu,Rui Cao,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QRC零样本框架，通过检索开放网络证据为多模态模因理解提供及时的背景知识，在多个数据集上优于零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多模态模因理解中常常忽视背景知识，或依赖预训练模型的固定参数化知识，这些知识可能不完整、过时或无法覆盖新兴模因。

Method: 引入Query Retrieve Conclude（QRC）零样本框架，包含三个步骤：识别缺失知识、检索开放网络证据、综合证据生成基于证据的背景知识用于模因理解和检测；并构建包含2024-2026年新模因及外部背景知识标注的评测基准。

Result: 在三个模因理解数据集和五个模因检测任务上的实验表明，该框架在知识恢复、模因理解和下游检测方面均优于零样本基线。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了动态模因理解中的知识获取问题，为及时理解新兴模因提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal memes are dynamic and often require up to date background knowledge for interpretation. Existing methods often overlook such knowledge or rely on fixed parametric knowledge of pretrained models that may be incomplete, outdated, or unavailable for emerging memes. We introduce Query Retrieve Conclude, a zero shot framework that identifies missing knowledge, retrieves open web evidence, and synthesizes evidence grounded background knowledge for meme understanding and detection. We also introduce a curated meme understanding benchmark of recent memes from 2024 to 2026 with external background knowledge annotations. Experiments on three meme understanding datasets and five meme detection tasks show that our framework improves knowledge recovery, meme understanding and downstream detection over zero shot baselines.

</details>


### [16] [GITCO: Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization in TSFMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05332)
*Manya Pandey,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GITCO框架，通过门控推理时上下文优化解决时间序列基础模型的上下文污染问题，在TimesFM 2.5上平均MASE降低1.95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于补丁的时间序列基础模型存在上下文污染问题：结构异常的补丁会捕获不成比例的注意力，进而默默降低零样本预测质量。现有方法难以在不修改模型权重的情况下解决该问题。

Method: 提出GITCO（门控推理时上下文优化）框架，包含门控、路由器和评论器三个轻量级组件，在不更新任何参数的情况下选择性地识别并抑制有害补丁；并引入上下文敏感度概貌作为TSFM的新特性。

Result: 在TimesFM 2.5模型上，使用53个GIFT-Eval数据集进行K折交叉验证，GITCO平均MASE降低+1.95%，同时捕获了89.9%的改进上界。

Conclusion: GITCO证明了在推理时优化输入上下文而非修改模型权重可以有效提升TSFM预测精度；上下文敏感度概貌揭示了模型架构与数据统计结构共同影响推理时上下文干预的效果。

Abstract: Patch-based Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) suffer from context poisoning: structurally anomalous patches capture disproportionate attention and silently degrade zero-shot forecast quality. We propose improving TSFM accuracy at inference time by optimizing the input context rather than modifying model weights. We present GITCO (Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization), a lightweight three-component framework: Gate, Router, and Critic that selectively identifies and suppresses harmful patches without any parameter updates. Evaluated on TimesFM 2.5 across 53 GIFT-Eval datasets under K-fold cross-validation, GITCO achieves an average +1.95% MASE reduction on TimesFM 2.5 while capturing 89.9% of the improvement upper bound. We introduce context sensitivity profiles as a new characterizable property of TSFMs: the mapping from time series meta-features to expected accuracy improvement under inference-time context intervention, shaped jointly by model architecture and the statistical structure of the data.

</details>


### [17] [Uncertainty Aware Functional Behavior Prediction and Material Fatigue Assessment for Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05334)
*Nehal Afifi,Mehdi Khabou,Victor Mas,Jonas Hemmerich,Patric Grauberger,Stefan Dietrich,Volker Schulze,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对循环工厂中退回产品的异质退化状态问题，提出了一个结合不确定性感知功能预测和组件级疲劳评估的实例特定可靠性框架。在角磨机上验证，功能变量预测达到96.52%的准确率，并成功整合功能、材料和系统可靠性轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环工厂中退回产品具有异质的退化状态、使用历史和剩余能力，仅凭当前检测无法决定再利用，因为未来功能履行和组件完整性在不同服务场景下可能演变不同。现有PHM方法多针对固定工况或孤立组件基准，且材料疲劳评估很少与系统级功能预测相关联。

Method: 提出一个针对角磨机的框架，包含两个并行分支：1) 使用卷积编码器从主轴力和轴扭矩中提取载荷模式，LSTM骨干网络预测九个功能变量（高斯均值和方差估计）；2) 通过有限元支持的应力重建、S-N/Miner损伤评估（Haibach扩展）和Paris法则裂纹生长分析，将载荷历史转化为输出轴疲劳信息。流式回放算法整合两个分支，生成功能、材料和系统可靠性轨迹。

Result: 在九个输出上达到平均2%容差准确率0.9652。热变量预测近乎完美，驱动电机电流和负载速度是最具挑战性的动态输出（R²分别为0.9750和0.9924）。扭矩历史对这些变量尤为重要，在短历史设置下传统LSTM优于GRU和xLSTM。可靠性校准对驱动电机电流最具信息价值。

Conclusion: 成功填补了功能预测与材料疲劳评估之间的空白，为循环工厂中退回产品的再利用决策提供了全面的可靠性评估方法，实现了实例特定的系统级可靠性预测。

Abstract: Returned products in circular factories re-enter production with heterogeneous degradation states, usage histories, and remaining capability. Reuse cannot be decided from the current inspection alone, because future function fulfillment and component integrity may evolve differently under the next service scenario. Existing PHM approaches support degradation prediction, but often target fixed operating conditions or isolated component benchmarks, while material-fatigue assessment is rarely linked to system-level functional prognosis. This paper addresses this gap for an angle grinder by combining uncertainty-aware functional prediction with component-level fatigue assessment in an instance-specific reliability workflow. The proposed framework combines the current tool state with recent force--torque usage windows. A convolutional encoder extracts loading patterns from spindle forces and shaft torque, and an LSTM backbone predicts nine functional variables as Gaussian mean and variance estimates. Torque history is especially important for these variables, and the conventional LSTM outperforms GRU and xLSTM in the short-history setting. Reliability calibration is most informative for drive motor current, where predicted and observed exceedance probabilities ...

### [18] [SentinelBench: A Benchmark for Long-Running Monitoring Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05342)
*Matheus Kunzler Maldaner,Adam Fourney,Amanda Swearngin,Hussein Mozzanar,Gagan Bansal,Maya Murad,Rafah Hosn,Saleema Amershi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SentinelBench基准，用于评估AI智能体在长时间监控任务中的表现，强调持续注意力策略优于持续行动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体在长时间任务中普遍采用持续行动模式（不断调用工具、刷新页面等），这对于需要等待外部事件的长时任务效率低下。作者认为智能体应采用持续注意力策略：监控环境、及时响应外部事件，在等待期间避免资源浪费。

Method: 提出SentinelBench开源基准，包含10个合成Web环境（邮件、日历、金融、职业网络、娱乐等）中的100个任务。每个环境提供实时Web界面并回放脚本化事件序列，要求智能体在页面状态动态变化中进行导航和推理。基准测量任务完成度、响应时间和资源使用量，揭示响应性与成本之间的权衡。

Result: 在三个模型和两个浏览器智能体框架上报告了基线结果，展示了智能体设计选择如何显著影响关键指标，证明SentinelBench能够区分智能体行为的有意义差异。

Conclusion: SentinelBench为评估长时间监控任务中的智能体行为提供了有效工具，能够测量响应性与成本之间的权衡，揭示了持续注意力策略在长时任务中的重要性，为未来智能体设计提供了评估基础。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly asked to carry out work that spans minutes, hours, or longer. Yet the default model of agent behavior is continuous action: issuing tool calls, refreshing pages, searching for alternatives, or otherwise trying to force progress. This is the wrong approach for many long-running tasks, which are better served by a strategy of sustained attention. Instead, agents should monitor an environment, notice when an external event makes progress possible, then respond promptly without wasting resources while waiting. To measure progress on this class of tasks, we introduce SentinelBench, an open-source benchmark for time-evolving monitoring tasks.
  SentinelBench contains 100 tasks across 10 synthetic web environments, including email, calendars, finance, professional networking, and entertainment. Each environment exposes a live web interface and replays a scripted sequence of events, requiring agents to navigate and reason about web pages whose state shifts underfoot. SentinelBench measures task completion, reaction time, and resource use, exposing the tradeoff between responsiveness and cost. We report results across three models and two browser-agent harnesses, establishing performance baselines for future comparison and demonstrating how agent design choices can dramatically impact key metrics. Together, these results show that SentinelBench distinguishes meaningful differences in agent behavior.

</details>

*Jincheng Yu,Haoyang Li,Yiwen Liu,Shen Liu,Rachel Yuanbao Chen,C. Kent Kwoh,Hongxu Ding,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个可解释且值得信赖的AI框架，结合深度学习预测MRI骨关节炎膝关节评分(MOAKS)与纵向潜在类别混合模型，发现骨 marrow 病变、软骨损失和半月板突出是骨关节炎疼痛进展的重要风险因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了开发一个可解释且值得信赖的AI框架，能够结合深度学习预测MRI骨关节炎膝关节评分(MOAKS)与可解释的统计建模，利用骨关节炎倡议(OAI)数据大规模研究结构与疼痛之间的关系。

Method: 首先开发深度学习框架直接从膝关节MRI预测MOAKS特征，并结合保形预测提供预测不确定性量化，这种不确定性感知策略能够明确过滤模型输出，仅保留膝关节水平的高置信度MOAKS预测。其次应用纵向潜在类别混合模型(LCMM)研究关键结构异常与四种互补膝关节疼痛测量之间的关联。

Result: 在三种MRI定义的异常(骨 marrow 病变(BML)、软骨损失(CART)和半月板突出(ME))中，框架显著提高了Matthews相关系数(MCC)等指标。例如，MCC从0.69提高到0.91(BML)、从0.45提高到0.80(CART)、从0.59提高到0.89(ME)。使用高置信度预测，将LCMM分析的样本量扩大到2,175个膝关节。识别出两种不同的疼痛轨迹(快速和稳定疼痛进展)。快速进展组的估计比值比(95% CI)分别为：BML 1.62(1.12-2.35)、CART损失1.83(1.24-2.70)、ME 2.50(1.75-3.57)。

Conclusion: 这些结果强调了这些结构异常作为骨关节炎疼痛和功能进展风险因素的重要性。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop an interpretable and trustworthy AI framework that combines deep learning based MRI Osteoarthritis Knee Score (MOAKS) prediction with interpretable statistical modeling to study structure-pain relationships at scale using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI). Materials and Methods: We first developed a deep learning framework to predict MOAKS features directly from knee MRIs and incorporated conformal prediction to provide prediction uncertainty quantification. This uncertainty-aware strategy enables explicit filtering of model outputs, retaining only high-confidence MOAKS predictions at the knee level. Second, we applied a longitudinal latent class mixed model (LCMM) to examine associations between key structural abnormalities and four complementary knee pain measurements. Results: Among the three MRI-defined abnormalities (i.e., bone marrow lesions (BML), cartilage loss (CART), and meniscal extrusion (ME)), our framework substantially improved the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) and some other metrics. For example, MCC increased from 0.69 to 0.91 for BML, from 0.45 to 0.80 for CART, and from 0.59 to 0.89 for ME. Using these high-confidence predictions, we expanded the sample size to 2,175 knees for the LCMM analysis. Two distinct pain trajectories were identified (rapid and stable pain progression). The estimated odds ratios (95% CI) for the rapid progression group were 1.62 (1.12-2.35) for BML, 1.83 (1.24-2.70) for CART loss, and 2.50 (1.75-3.57) for ME. Conclusion: These results highlight the importance of these structural abnormalities as risk factors for pain and functional progression in osteoarthritis.

</details>


### [20] [Synthetic Contrastive Reasoning for Multi-Table Q&A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05382)
*Ankit Pratap Singh,Xin Su,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建合成对比推理轨迹数据集并使用对比偏好优化(CPO)微调开放权重LLMs，在多表问答任务上相比监督微调实现了9.7%-16.3%的绝对平均提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多表问答资源通常仅提供问题和最终答案，缺乏解释答案如何推导的推理监督信号，这限制了模型在跨关系表的组合推理能力。

Method: 构建MMQA的合成对比推理轨迹数据集，使用异构LLMs生成验证的正向轨迹和合理的负向轨迹，利用生成的偏好对通过对比偏好优化(CPO)微调开放权重LLMs。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B、Mistral-8B和Llama-3.1-8B三个模型上，CPO相比问答监督微调实现了9.7%-16.3%的绝对平均提升，在MMQA上最高提升达21个百分点；消融实验表明异构正负轨迹生成器增强了对比信号。

Conclusion: 异构正负轨迹生成器能有效增强对比信号，自动和人工评估表明生成的偏好对在忠实性、连贯性和有意义的对比性方面表现良好，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-table question answering requires models to retrieve relevant evidence, link schemas, and perform compositional reasoning across relational tables. Existing multi-table Q&A resources typically provide questions and final answers but lack reasoning supervision that explains how answers are derived. To address this gap, we construct a synthetic contrastive reasoning-trace dataset for MMQA by generating validated positive traces and plausible negative traces with heterogeneous LLMs. We then use the resulting preference pairs to fine-tune open-weight LLMs with Contrastive Preference Optimization (CPO). Across Qwen3-14B, Mistral-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B, CPO achieves absolute average improvements over Q&A supervised fine-tuning ranging from 9.7%-16.3%, with gains up to 21 percentage points on MMQA. Ablations show that heterogeneous positive and negative trace generators strengthen the contrastive signal, and automated as well as human evaluations indicate that the generated pairs are largely faithful, coherent, and meaningfully contrastive.

</details>


### [21] [Stability vs. Manipulability: Evaluating Robustness Under Post-Decision Interaction in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05384)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM作为评判者的评估结果在交互过程中不具备稳定性，即使初始判断稳定，也能通过后续有针对性的对话挑战被逆转，导致与人类偏好一致性下降、基准排名改变等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-judge评估流程通常假设判断结果是固定输入的稳定属性，作者旨在验证这一假设在交互场景下是否成立，特别是在初始决策做出后，评估结果是否会被后续对话操纵。

Method: 在MT-Bench和AlpacaEval上进行对照实验，研究“决策后可操纵性”，即通过后续对话改变初始评估结果的程度。使用反基线挑战协议展示稳定判断如何被动机性交互推翻，并使用平衡目标验证协议分离可逆性与净目标导向操纵。同时引入评估鲁棒性分数(ERS)量化交互鲁棒性。

Result: LLM评判者在重复中性重评下高度稳定，但在针对性决策后挑战下显著可逆。逆转会降低与人类偏好的一致性、改变基准排名、产生有害评估变化。权威框架特别容易导致不稳定，修订后的判断常伴随低重叠度的理由，暗示事后合理化而非可靠的错误修正。

Conclusion: 决策后交互是LLM-as-judge评估的独特失效模式，现有评估协议不应仅测量静态一致性，还需测量在挑战下的鲁棒性，以更全面地评估LLM作为评判者的可靠性。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge evaluation is widely used in benchmarking pipelines, where model outputs are compared and ranked using automated evaluators. These pipelines typically assume that judgments are stable properties of fixed inputs. We show that this assumption does not hold under interaction. We study post-decision manipulability: the extent to which an evaluation outcome can be altered through subsequent conversation with the judge after an initial decision has been made. Across controlled experiments on MT-Bench and AlpacaEval, we find that LLM judges are highly stable under repeated and neutral reevaluation, yet become substantially reversible under targeted post-decision challenge. An anti-baseline challenge protocol shows that stable judgments can be overturned through motivated interaction, while a counterbalanced target-validation protocol separates this reversibility from net target-directed steering. These reversals have practical consequences: they can degrade agreement with human preferences, shift benchmark rankings, and produce harmful evaluation changes despite high self-reported confidence. Authority framing is especially destabilizing, and revised judgments are often accompanied by low-overlap justifications, suggesting post hoc rationalization rather than reliable error correction. We introduce the Evaluation Robustness Score (ERS) to quantify interactional robustness by combining reversal susceptibility with counterbalanced directional effects. Our findings identify post-decision interaction as a distinct failure mode for LLM-as-judge evaluation and motivate evaluation protocols that measure not only static agreement, but robustness under challenge.

</details>


### [22] [Residual Modeling for High-Fidelity Learned Compression of Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05389)
*Liangji Zhu,Sanjay Ranka,Anand Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对高保真度场景下已有GAE方法残差修正数据流占比过大的问题，提出LBRC和NGLR两种残差编码器，在NRMSE为10^-6到10^-4时压缩率显著优于GAE，并可与SZ竞争或超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在学习压缩器的高保真度应用中（块级NRMSE为10^-6至10^-4），现有GAE方法需保留大量SVD/PCA系数进行残差校正，导致校正数据流占比急剧上升并主导总比特率，抵消了学习压缩的优势。

Method: 提出以残差为中心的观点，认为学习残差与原始科学场具有不同结构特性。设计了两种残差编码器：LBRC是无训练的确定性流水线，自适应量化学习残差并通过3D Lorenzo差分、Zigzag映射、位平面编码和熵编码进行无损压缩；NGLR在此基础上加入因果神经预测器，输出用于整数Lorenzo预测的归一化偏置，在保持确定性解码的同时降低残差码的熵。

Result: 在E3SM、JHTDB和ERA5数据集上的实验表明，在块级NRMSE目标10^-6至10^-4的高保真度区间，LBRC相比GAE提升压缩率30-60%，与SZ方法竞争力相当；NGLR在LBRC基础上进一步提升10-40%，并在该保真度区间超越SZ。

Conclusion: 针对学习压缩器残差特性设计的专用残差表示方法，能够在全局残差校正确率占比过高时保持学习压缩的优势，显著提升高保真度场景下的压缩效率。

Abstract: Lossy compression is essential for massive spatiotemporal data from scientific simulations. Learned compressors can achieve high compression ratios at moderate accuracy targets, but their aggregate reconstruction losses do not guarantee accuracy for each block. Existing Guaranteed Autoencoder (GAE) methods add a per-block residual correction by retaining SVD/PCA-style coefficients until the target is met. This works at moderate tolerances, but in the high-fidelity regime with block-level NRMSE from 10^-6 to 10^-4, the number of retained coefficients grows quickly and the correction stream dominates the total rate.
  We propose a residual-centric view: the learned residual is structurally different from the original scientific field and should be coded with a representation designed for that residual. We introduce two residual coders. LBRC is a deterministic, training-free pipeline that adaptively quantizes the learned residual to the target NRMSE and losslessly encodes the resulting integer residual using 3D Lorenzo differencing, zigzag mapping, bit-plane coding, and entropy coding. NGLR adds a causal neural predictor that outputs a normalized bias for an integer-rounded Lorenzo prediction in the same deterministic integer pipeline, reducing the entropy of the remaining residual code while preserving deterministic decoding. The predictor weights are serialized and counted in the bitstream.
  Across E3SM, JHTDB, and ERA5 at block-level NRMSE targets from 10^-6 to 10^-4, LBRC improves compression ratio over GAE by 30-60% and is broadly competitive with SZ. NGLR adds a further 10-40% over LBRC and outperforms SZ in the evaluated high-fidelity regime. These results show that residual representations tailored to learned-compressor residuals can preserve the advantage of learned compression when global residual correction becomes rate-dominant.

</details>


### [23] [LeanMarathon: Toward Reliable AI Co-Mathematicians through Long-Horizon Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05400)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Yuekai Sun,Taiji Suzuki,Jason D. Lee,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了LeanMarathon，一个用于研究级Lean自动形式化的多智能体框架，通过演化蓝图和协调智能体解决了长期自动形式化中的规模和可靠性问题，在四个Erdős问题上成功形式化了7个目标定理和258个引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期自动形式化研究数学不仅因困难引理失败，更因规模问题而失败：陈述漂移、依赖纠缠、上下文衰减和局部修复破坏远程工作。现有的AI数学系统缺乏在长数学开发过程中保持目标保真度的持久机制。

Method: LeanMarathon采用多智能体架构，核心抽象是演化蓝图——一个同时作为形式证明骨架、自然语言证明图和共享记录系统的Lean文件。四个契约范围智能体负责构造、审计、证明和修复蓝图。两阶段协调器首先通过对抗性审查稳定目标保真度，然后从动态叶节点向上以并行CI门控轮次释放证明有向无环图(DAG)。这种方法将一次脆弱的多小时运行转化为多个局部、可恢复、并行的事务。

Result: 在两篇涉及四个Erdős问题(#1051, #1196, #164, #1217)的最新研究论文上评估LeanMarathon。在三次自主运行中，成功形式化所有七个目标定理，无sorry，证明了258个引理和定理。代码已在https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon开源。

Conclusion: 可靠的AI协作数学不仅需要更强的证明器，更需要能够在长数学开发过程中保持目标保真度的持久框架。LeanMarathon展示了通过多智能体协调和蓝图抽象实现可靠研究级自动形式化的可行性。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoformalization of research mathematics fails not only at hard lemmas, but at scale: statements drift, dependencies tangle, context decays, and local repairs corrupt distant work. We present LeanMarathon, a multi-agent harness for reliable research-level Lean autoformalization. Its core abstraction is an evolving blueprint: a Lean file that serves simultaneously as formal proof skeleton, natural-language proof graph, and shared system of record. Four contract-scoped agents construct, audit, prove, and repair this blueprint. These agents are coordinated by a two-stage orchestrator that first stabilizes target fidelity through adversarial review and then discharges the proof directed acyclic graph (DAG) from its dynamic leaves upward in parallel CI-gated rounds. LeanMarathon turns one brittle multi-hour run into many local, recoverable, parallel transactions. We evaluate LeanMarathon on two recent research papers spanning four Erdős problems (#1051, #1196, #164, #1217). Across three autonomous runs, it formalizes all seven target theorems with no sorry, proving 258 lemmas and theorems. These results show that reliable AI co-mathematics requires not only stronger provers, but durable harnesses that preserve target fidelity across long mathematical developments. The code can be found at https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon.

</details>


### [24] [Harnessing Generalist Agents for Contextualized Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05404)
*Zihao Li,Kaifeng Jin,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Avaneesh Kumar,Xuying Ning,Yanjun Zhao,Mengting Ai,Baoyu Jing,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TimeClaw，一个面向时间序列的代理框架，通过集成可执行时间工具、经验驱动的演化能力和情景多模态记忆，赋能通用LLM智能体进行具备上下文感知的时间推理，在多个领域的基准测试中展现出性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列通常嵌入在丰富的上下文环境中，这对于整体建模至关重要。然而，现有的通用AI智能体主要在文本空间中操作，与结构化时间信号未能充分对齐。同时，现实世界从业者需要端到端的工作流，而预测等广泛研究的任务仅是更大解决循环中的一个步骤，缺乏支持复杂上下文和结构化时序数据的统一框架。

Method: 提出TimeClaw框架，核心包含三个组件：（1）可执行时间工具，提供可落地、可审计的分析能力；（2）经验驱动的演化能力，创建可复用的分析流程；（3）情景多模态记忆，检索相关的推理轨迹。这些组件协同工作，使通用LLM代理能够在结构化运行时环境中进行上下文感知的开放性时间推理。

Result: 在覆盖能源、金融、天气、交通等多个真实世界领域的多个基准测试上进行了广泛评估，TimeClaw展现出显著性能提升，验证了框架在多样化时序任务上的有效性和普适性。

Conclusion: TimeClaw成功弥合了通用LLM智能体与时间序列原生分析之间的鸿沟，为端到端的上下文感知时序分析工作流提供了有效解决方案。该框架通过工具集成、经验演化和记忆机制的结合，为实现更具解释性和可复用性的时序智能分析开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Time series are often embedded in rich contexts that are essential for holistic modeling. Moreover, real-world practitioners often require end-to-end workflows for analyzing temporal dynamics, where widely studied tasks such as forecasting are only one step in a broader solution loop. While generalist AI agents offer a promising interface for such workflows under complex contexts, they still operate primarily in textual spaces that are not fully aligned with structured temporal signals. In this work, we introduce TimeClaw, an agentic harness framework for time series that equips generalist LLM agents with the time series-native runtime support needed for contextualized temporal reasoning. TimeClaw integrates executable temporal tools for grounded and auditable analysis, experience-driven capability evolution for creating reusable analytical routines, and episodic multimodal memory for retrieving relevant reasoning traces. Together, these components unlock harnessed open-ended temporal reasoning with contextual information. Extensive evaluation on multiple benchmarks covering diverse tasks across energy, finance, weather, traffic, and other real-world domains demonstrates improved performance of TimeClaw. Code is available at https://github.com/iDEA-iSAIL-Lab-UIUC/TimeClaw.

</details>

Liu,Zimeng Chen,Weiyan Shi,Amanda Dsouza,Vincent Sunn Chen,Patrick Bryant,Carl Boettiger,Yamini Rangan,Bradley Rothenberg,Kyle Steinfeld,Arvind Rao,Tapio Schneider,Georgios Yannakakis,Laure Zanna,Kaan
